Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a biochemistry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 815. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 796/3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to biochemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 815 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 796. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
